TL;DR: LM-X是一种受生物传感器运动控制启发的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略模型，通过引入任务、事件和运动尺度上的显式预测信号（如return-to-go、event-to-go和异方差动作流），实现多时间尺度的可解释控制，在仿真和真实机器人任务中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的通用VLA策略主要依赖短视界动作预测，缺乏对任务进展、中间意图和局部可靠性的显式建模，导致控制过程中关键状态隐含且不可解释，形成性能与可解释性双重瓶颈。

Method: 提出LM-X模型，引入三个在线显式监督信号：return-to-go（RTG）衡量任务进展，event-to-go（ETG）识别下一个语义状态转移，异方差动作流通过传播方差估计局部可靠性；这些信号直接调节动作生成，使解释成为控制的内在部分。

Result: 在50个随机困难的RoboTwin2.0任务上，LM-X达到74.1%的成功率，显著高于GR00T N1.7的55.4%；在七个真实机器人任务上达到68.6%，优于基线的50.7%；同时RTG能准确反映语义进度，方差信号在犹豫和振荡控制时上升，验证了内部状态的可解释性。

Conclusion: 显式的多时间尺度预测状态不仅提升了长视野控制性能，还提供了内在可解释性，为未来机器人策略学习提供了更透明、可控的新范式。

Abstract: Generalist vision--language--action (VLA) policies learn long-horizon behavior mainly through short-horizon action prediction and reveal little beyond sampled commands. This creates two coupled bottlenecks: a single action target must implicitly absorb task progress, intermediate intent, and local reliability, while these control states remain hidden during execution. Inspired by functional principles of biological sensorimotor control, we introduce LM-X , which organizes prediction across task, event, and motor scales without claiming anatomical correspondence. Three explicitly supervised signals are emitted online and directly condition action generation: return-to-go (RTG) measures visible task progress, event-to-go (ETG) identifies the next semantic transition, and heteroscedastic action flow estimates local reliability through propagated variance. Explanation is therefore intrinsic to control rather than generated post hoc. Before a costly 20-day pretraining run on 64 NVIDIA B200 GPUs, a controlled five-task pretraining gate verifies the design: the complete model improves success by 16.0 points over the action-only backbone and by 10.8 points over the strongest single-head variant. We then train LM-X on more than 20,000 hours of real-robot trajectories, including over 1,000 hours of failed policy rollouts. LM-X achieves 74.1\% across 50 randomized-hard RoboTwin2.0 tasks versus 55.4\% for GR00T N1.7, and 68.6\% versus 50.7\% across seven real-robot tasks. RTG tracks semantic progress and visible regression, while variance rises during hesitation and oscillatory control. These results show that explicit multi-timescale predictive state can strengthen control while exposing interpretable internal estimates.

</details>


### [187] [TacForcing: Streaming Action Generation with Execution-Time Tactile Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25798)
*Jianbo Zhou,Boyuan Zhao,Yuzheng Zhang,Yiyang Chen,Wenxin Chen,Qiuyue Li,Xiangyang Gu,Yuhan Cao,Xiao Xia,Yanzhe Hu,Zhijie Deng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TacForcing的流式动作生成框架，通过执行过程中的触觉反馈实时调整动作，提升了接触丰富操作任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于块的视觉-语言-动作模型无法在执行过程中更新触觉信息，而触觉响应方法通常依赖独立的高频控制器，增加了系统复杂性。因此需要一种更高效、集成的触觉反馈机制。

Method: 提出TacForcing框架，使用流式动作专家替代传统动作专家，并引入执行感知触觉注意力（EATA）机制，使动作生成依赖于执行中不断更新的触觉观测，减少触觉获取与动作执行之间的时间错配。

Result: 在六个模拟UniVTAC任务和三个真实世界接触丰富操作任务上，TacForcing分别实现了65%和69%的平均成功率，优于多种强基线方法。

Conclusion: TacForcing通过流式动作生成和EATA机制，有效整合了执行时触觉反馈，在不增加架构复杂性的前提下显著提升了控制性能，适用于多种接触丰富的操作场景。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires adapting to contact states that can evolve substantially within an action horizon. However, chunk-based vision-language-action models predict complete action chunks from observations collected before execution, leaving tactile conditioning stale during execution. Existing tactile-reactive approaches typically rely on separate high-frequency controllers, which increase both architectural and training complexity. In this paper, we introduce TacForcing, a streaming action-generation framework that effectively incorporates execution-time tactile feedback. Instead of employing a separate reactive controller, TacForcing replaces the standard action expert with a streaming action expert to generate actions conditioned on the evolving tactile observations acquired during execution. TacForcing also introduces Execution-Aware Tactile Attention (EATA), which restricts tactile conditioning to actions nearing execution, thereby reducing the temporal mismatch between tactile acquisition and action execution. Across six simulated UniVTAC tasks and three real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, TacForcing achieves average success rates of 65% and 69%, respectively, outperforming strong baselines in both settings.

</details>


### [188] [AGRO-Nav: Autonomous Graph-based Orchard Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25799)
*Ho Young Yun,Jaemin Yu,Duksu Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为AGRO-Nav的自动化静态图全局路径规划框架，专用于果园环境中的导航。该方法基于SLAM点云中拟合的树行线构建稀疏拓扑图，并结合Dijkstra、Theta*和B样条平滑生成精确且平滑的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 果园环境中树行平行但通道狭窄、枝叶密集，传统的网格规划方法容易偏离行中心，存在碰撞风险。因此需要一种更精确、鲁棒的全局规划方法。

Method: 利用SLAM点云中聚类的树干拟合出行道线，自动构建包含行内和行间连接关系的稀疏拓扑图；使用Dijkstra算法在图上搜索全局路径，通过Theta*任意角度段连接起点和终点，并用立方B样条进行路径平滑。

Result: 在真实果园测试中，AGRO-Nav的平均路径误差为0.08米，显著低于A*（0.31米）和Theta*（0.43米）基线方法，规划速度提升约4到5倍；在Isaac Sim仿真中也表现最优，且在树木密度降至70%时仍保持稳定。

Conclusion: AGRO-Nav能够在无需人工路点的情况下实现高精度、高效率的果园导航，生成的轨迹适合差速驱动和四轮转向平台，具有良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Orchards form semi-structured environments in which parallel tree rows create natural driving corridors, yet narrow inter-row clearance and dense foliage lead geometry-agnostic grid planners to drift off the row center and risk trunk or canopy contact. We present AGRO-Nav, an automated framework for static graph-based global planning in orchards. From tree-row lines fitted to trunk clusters in a SLAM point cloud, it builds, without any manual waypoints, a sparse topological graph of intra- and inter-row connectivity; a global route is then found by Dijkstra search on this graph, connected to the start and goal by any-angle Theta* segments, and smoothed with a cubic B-spline. In real-orchard trials, AGRO-Nav follows the row center with a mean error of about 0.08 m, far below the A* (0.31 m) and Theta* (0.43 m) shortest-path baselines, while planning roughly four to five times faster. In Isaac Sim, it attains the lowest error among A*, Theta*, and a reproduced RANSAC midline baseline and remains stable as tree density drops to 70%, where the RANSAC baseline degrades. The resulting trajectories---straight row-centered segments joined by controlled turns---suit differential-drive and four-wheel-steering platforms.

</details>


### [189] [Anytime Global Tensor Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25830)
*Sai Coumar,An T. Le,Zachary Kingston*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种广义的全局张量运动规划（GTMP）方法，通过分层多部图上的批处理张量操作实现，并支持任意黑箱局部规划器。该方法具备两种任意时间策略，能够在保证覆盖所有同伦类的同时收敛到最优解，在操作和导航任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升运动规划的灵活性与效率，需要一种能够整合多种局部规划器并支持批量、多样化路径生成的通用框架。

Method: 将GTMP推广为使用任意黑箱局部规划器构建相邻层之间的边，并设计了两种任意时间策略：固定预算下的随机重启Anytime GTMP，以及随预算增长的启发式扩展AO-GTMP。

Result: 理论证明单个采样图几乎必然覆盖所有具有有界长度δ-清晰代表元的端点固定同伦类；每层额外采样可指数级降低未命中概率，而更强的局部规划器仅亚线性减少所需层数。实验显示该方法在操作任务中达到最先进水平，在2D导航中生成拓扑多样化的路径批次。

Conclusion: 广义GTMP提供了一个灵活高效的运动规划框架，结合任意局部规划器与批量张量计算，兼具完备性、最优性与多样性，在多种场景中表现优异。

Abstract: Global Tensor Motion Planning (GTMP) solves motion planning with batched tensor operations over a layered multipartite graph. We generalize GTMP so that adjacent-layer edges are realized by any black-box local planner (e.g., linear interpolation, splines, sampling-based planning, trajectory optimization, or generative sampling). We provide two anytime policies on top of this generalization: Anytime GTMP with random restarts at a fixed budget, which covers every homotopy class almost surely, and AO-GTMP with informed expansion with growing budgets, which converges to the optimal cost. We prove that a single sampled graph covers every endpoint-fixed homotopy class admitting a \(δ\)-clear representative of bounded length. We also prove that additional samples per layer reduce the per-layer miss probability exponentially, whereas stronger local planners reduce the required layer count only sublinearly. On manipulation benchmarks the method matches state-of-the-art performance, and on 2D navigation it returns batches of topologically diverse solutions, while the informed baselines concentrate on one or two classes.

</details>


### [190] [MA-VLA: Multi-Arm Vision-Language-Action Model for Collaboration and Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25864)
*Zaibin Zhang,Junlan Xiao,Zhongbo Zhang,Yifan Wang,Li Kang,Yiran Qin,Changxing Xia,Heng Zhou,Talas Fu,Enshen Zhou,Ruimao Zhang,Zhenfei Yin,Huchuan Lu,Lijun Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多臂协作的统一框架MA-VLA，通过将合作行为分解为中层原子提示并分配给各个机械臂，实现对子目标的显式指定和任务间的组合复用。引入训练时的Arm Shuffle机制，增强了角色无关的指令跟随能力，并支持在测试时生成未见过的协作模式。实验表明，相比现有最先进模型，MA-VLA在仿真和真实场景下均展现出更强的多臂组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型大多使用全局语言指令，缺乏对各机械臂行为的显式分配机制，限制了在训练中未见协作模式下的迁移能力。因此需要一种能够支持灵活、可组合多臂协作的框架。

Method: 提出MA-VLA框架，将协作任务分解为中层原子动作提示，并为每个机械臂显式分配对应提示；引入Arm Shuffle，在训练时随机置换各机械臂的观测、状态和提示，强制模型学习角色无关的策略，提升泛化能力。

Result: 在构建的新基准上进行评估，现有SOTA VLA模型在未见协作模式下表现不佳，而MA-VLA在仿真和真实环境中均能成功完成任务，验证了其在多臂组合泛化方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化的、按机械臂分配原子动作的方法为多臂具身系统提供了可扩展的泛化路径，MA-VLA结合原子动作分配与Arm Shuffle机制，显著提升了在新协作模式下的适应能力。

Abstract: Multi-arm collaboration is becoming a core capability in embodied manipulation. Recent vision-language-action (VLA) models integrate perception, language, and control, but most represent language as a single global instruction and do not provide an explicit mechanism for assigning and composing arm-specific behaviors. This design limits transfer to collaboration patterns that differ from those observed during training. We present MA-VLA, a unified framework for multi-arm collaboration via atomic action assignment. MA-VLA decomposes cooperative behavior into mid-level atomic prompts and allocates them to individual arms, enabling explicit subgoal specification and compositional reuse across tasks. To reduce reliance on fixed execution roles, we introduce Arm Shuffle, a training-time permutation of the observation, state, and assigned atomic prompts for each arm. This permutation enforces role-agnostic instruction following and supports recomposition into unseen coordination patterns, which we term multi-arm compositional generalization. We also construct a benchmark in which test-time collaboration patterns are absent in training set. Across simulation and real-world evaluations, prior state-of-the-art VLAs largely fail under these unseen collaborations, while MA-VLA consistently succeeds. These results indicate that structured, per-arm atomic action assignment offers a practical route to scalable generalization in multi-arm embodied systems. Code, models, and data are available at https://github.com/zhangzaibin/future-robots

</details>


### [191] [VISTA: Visually Inferred Spatial ConTact Attention for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25872)
*Jiayi Chen,Wenlong Dong,Yan Huang,Xianglin Chen,Zijian Lin,Jiaqi Yin,Yushan Liu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VISTA-Policy，利用视觉变形场（VDF）作为高维视觉-物理反馈信号，实现无需额外触觉硬件的精确接触感知，在多种精细操作任务中超越纯视觉和触觉基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模仿学习在接触状态感知上存在模糊性，而专用触觉/力传感器又增加硬件复杂性和成本，因此需要一种无需额外传感器即可获取丰富接触信息的方法。

Method: 提出VISTA-Policy框架，包含：1）物理感知编码引擎用于实时解码VDF；2）能量聚合去噪机制以提取真实交互信号；3）变形增强策略网络结合增量动作实现闭环控制。

Result: 在跨尺度抓取、开盖和书法书写任务中表现优于3D Diffusion Policy和触觉基线，具备对未见物体尺度的良好泛化能力和抗动态干扰的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: VISTA-Policy通过利用柔性夹爪的视觉变形场提供了一种耐用且低成本的通用精细操作解决方案，适用于非结构化环境中的复杂接触操作。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires precise interaction feedback. While vision-centric imitation learning is prevalent, external visual observations provide indirect and ambiguous cues about contact states, particularly under occlusion or subtle object--gripper interactions; dedicated tactile or force sensors can provide rich contact information but introduce additional hardware complexity, calibration requirements, and deployment costs. To bridge this gap, we propose VISTA-Policy, an imitation learning paradigm that utilizes the Visual Deformation Field (VDF), a 3D displacement representation of a compliant gripper, as high-dimensional visuo-physical feedback. The framework integrates: 1) a Physics-Aware Encoding Engine for real-time VDF decoding; 2) an Energy Aggregation Denoising Mechanism to isolate true interaction signals; and 3) a Deformation-Augmented Policy Network with incremental gripper actions for precise closed-loop correction. Extensive evaluations on Cross-Scale Object Grasping, Cap Unscrewing, and Calligraphy Writing demonstrate that VISTA-Policy outperforms the strong pure-vision baseline 3D Diffusion Policy and the tactile baseline. VISTA-Policy further demonstrates substantial out-of-distribution generalization to unseen object scales and robustness against dynamic disturbances, offering a durable and cost-effective route toward general-purpose fine-grained manipulation in unstructured environments. Project videos and supplementary materials are available at: https://sites.google.com/view/vista-policy.

</details>


### [192] [Low-Resolution Perception for Robotic Packing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25874)
*Giuseppe Fabio Preziosa,Federico Vignoni,Chiara Castellano,Marco Faroni,Andrea Maria Zanchettin,Paolo Rocco*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于低分辨率深度感知的机器人装箱可扩展感知框架，通过重建线索和抓取证据联合决策下一步操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低成本、低分辨率深度传感在机器人装箱中实现可扩展感知的问题。

Method: 采用由重建线索驱动的下一最佳视图（NBV）策略，避免冗余视角并保留任务相关几何信息，并利用抓取证据更新物体稳定性估计。

Result: 在极低分辨率下验证了效用函数的有效性，并通过端到端实验评估了不同策略下的性能，表明低分辨率感知是机器人装箱的实用且可扩展的方案。

Conclusion: 低分辨率深度感知结合智能视图选择与稳定性估计，能够有效支持大规模机器人装箱应用。

Abstract: This work tackles the problem of scalable perception for robotic packing with low-cost, low-resolution depth sensing. We propose a framework where reconstruction cues drive next-view selection and grasp evidence updates a per-object stability estimate, jointly deciding what to acquire next and when to grasp. During the reconstruction, a low-resolution Next Best View (NBV) strategy explicitly avoids redundant views while preserving task-relevant geometry. We validate the approach in two steps: (i) an ablation study of the utility function under very low resolution, and (ii) a full end-to-end evaluation across policies, showing how low-resolution perception is a practical, scalable option for robotic packing.

</details>


### [193] [A Statistical Audit of Physical AI Benchmark Redundancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25940)
*Zaruhi Navasardyan,Hrant Davtyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个包含51个模型和12个物理AI基准的评估矩阵，发现基准间存在显著冗余，并提出一种贪心方法选择最小基准子集，在保留大部分评估效用的同时简化评测流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理AI模型评估使用的基准不统一，导致模型与基准之间的比较稀疏，且基准间的相关性和信息重叠缺乏量化分析。因此需要系统性地评估基准间的冗余性并优化基准选择。

Method: 从51个基准和152个模型中筛选出报告密度高的12个基准和51个模型构建评估矩阵；计算基准间的信息共享程度以衡量冗余性；采用贪心算法选择能最大化得分离散性和独立性的基准子集，并使用Bradley-Terry模型进行排名。

Result: 发现多个基准之间存在显著冗余，合并两个可替代的基准对会导致22个模型排名变动超过三位；提出的四基准子集保留了全部12个基准78.5%的评估效用。

Conclusion: 物理AI基准测试中存在大量冗余，可通过信息驱动的方法精简基准集合，在不显著损失评估质量的前提下降低评测成本，该方法具有跨领域的通用性。

Abstract: Physical AI models are evaluated on suites of benchmarks that differ across model reports, leaving the model-by-benchmark matrix sparse and the relationship between benchmarks unmeasured. We construct a matrix of 51 models on 12 physical AI benchmarks, selected from a registry of 51 benchmarks and 152 models by reporting density, combining scores from model cards and benchmark papers with our own evaluation runs under each benchmark's official protocol. We measure how much information the benchmarks share and show quantitative evidence of Redundancy. Redundancy affects reported rankings: collapsing the two substitute pairs into single columns moves 22 of 51 models by three or more places under an equally weighted average. We then select benchmarks greedily under a utility combining score dispersion with variance not explained by the already-selected set, and obtain a four-benchmark subset retaining 78.5\% of the utility of all 12, on which we fit a Bradley--Terry ranking. The procedure requires only benchmark-level scores with sufficient overlap and is not specific to physical AI.

</details>


### [194] [DESCENT: Directed Edge Scene Encoding for Airport Surface Movement Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26002)
*Alexander Prutsch,David Schinagl,Horst Possegger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer的架构DESCENT，用于机场地面运动的轨迹预测，通过引入潜在可达集（PRS）上下文采样机制，在复杂操作环境下实现了更准确、安全的长期预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机场地面运动的轨迹预测在航空安全中至关重要，但现有研究较少，尤其是在应对异构动力学和严格拓扑约束方面缺乏有效方法。

Method: 设计了基于Transformer的DESCENT架构，结合潜在可达集（PRS）自适应上下文采样机制和检测Transformer解码器，以捕捉不同运行阶段的环境上下文并生成轨迹预测。

Result: 在Amelia-10基准上的实验表明，DESCENT显著优于现有最先进基线，尤其在安全关键场景中表现出更强的长时域预测能力。

Conclusion: DESCENT通过领域感知的上下文建模有效提升了机场地面运动预测的准确性与安全性，为航空地勤自动化提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Advanced automation is a key technology for enhancing the safety of ground operations amidst the increasing density of commercial air traffic. While motion forecasting is a well-studied task in autonomous driving, its application to airport surface movements remains underexplored. To enable efficient and accurate prediction in this domain, we propose DESCENT, a transformer-based architecture designed to handle heterogeneous dynamics and strict topological constraints. Our approach features a Potential Reachable Set (PRS) context sampling mechanism that adaptively collects airfield environment context across diverse operational phases. Combined with a detection transformer-based decoder, DESCENT generates accurate trajectory forecasts. Extensive evaluations on the Amelia-10 benchmark demonstrate significant performance improvements over state-of-the-art baselines. These gains are especially pronounced in safety-critical scenarios, where our domain-aware sampling provides critical long-horizon context necessary for safe navigation.

</details>


### [195] [Phantom Navigator: Stealthy and Precise Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Redirection with Real-Time Tracking and GPS Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26011)
*Haocheng Meng,Shaocheng Luo,Songqiao Xie,Miroslav Pajic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Phantom Navigator的无人机重定向攻击方法，通过结合离线可达性分析和在线闭环隐蔽执行，实现对无人机的精确、隐蔽重定向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机重定向攻击在可靠性、精度和隐蔽性方面存在不足，难以实现针对特定目标的有效引导。

Method: 提出Phantom Navigator，结合离线预重定向可达性分析与在线闭环隐蔽执行层，并构建包含LiDAR-相机检测、跟踪与欺骗系统的物理攻击平台，实现实时识别、位姿估计与欺骗信号计算。

Result: 在真实场景案例中验证了该方法的有效性和攻击实现的可行性，能够隐蔽且准确地将受害无人机引导至指定位置。

Conclusion: Phantom Navigator能够在无需物理捕获或通信入侵的情况下，以高精度和强隐蔽性实现对无人机的定向误导，显著优于传统方法。

Abstract: Redirecting unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) from their intended mission trajectories has been an active area of research. However, existing UAV redirection attacks lack reliability, precision, and covertness for a targeted diversion. They primarily rely on physical capture, communication hijacking, or sensor spoofing. Yet, physical interception is costly, offers only a single opportunity for success, and poses a high risk of collateral damage; network-based attacks demand deep technical expertise and access to encrypted communication channels; and sensor spoofing techniques typically fall short in achieving the accuracy and robustness required to steer a UAV toward a specified target. Consequently, we propose Phantom Navigator, a UAV redirection attack to mislead drones to a designated spoofing target, covertly and precisely. Our approach combines offline pre-redirection reachability analysis, which provides high-fidelity estimates of achievable redirect ranges, with an online closed-loop, stealthy execution layer that ensures successful redirection in practice. Based on this approach, we build a physical attack platform equipped with a LiDAR--camera detection, tracking, and spoofing stack that performs real-time identification, pose estimation, and computation of targeted spoofing signals to covertly and accurately redirect victim UAVs to a designated location. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our redirection methodology and the attack implementation in real-world case studies.

</details>


### [196] [When Obstacles Bend: Modeling Vegetation Deformation in the context of Field Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26050)
*Muhammad Hsaeeb Zaar Khizar,Tom Montagnon,Roland Lenain,Romuald Aufrère,Johann Laconte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于植被内在机械特性（而非机器人平台依赖指标）的表征方法，通过结合形变测量与接触力数据估计植被力学参数，实现对植被响应的重建，从而支持植被感知的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可通行性评估将环境特性与机器人动力学耦合，难以跨平台迁移，且未反映植被本身的属性，限制了在农业和环境监测中的应用。

Method: 结合植被的形变测量与接触力数据，估计其内在机械参数，独立于具体机器人平台地重建植被对交互的响应。

Result: 实现了基于植被内在特性的可通行性表征，支持更通用、可迁移的植被感知导航。

Conclusion: 通过解耦机器人动力学与环境特性，所提方法能够更准确地反映植被本质属性，为多平台共享的智能导航提供基础。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating in natural environments must often interact with vegetation rather than simply avoid it. In this context, traversability is typically defined from the robot's perspective, by measuring how a specific platform responds when moving through the environment. While practical, this viewpoint entangles the assessment of the environment with the robot's own dynamics, making the resulting characterization difficult to transfer across different platforms. More importantly, it does not directly reflect the properties of the vegetation itself, which are the true source of interaction and potential damage in applications such as agriculture and environmental monitoring. To address this limitation, we propose to characterize vegetation through its intrinsic mechanical properties, independently of any specific robot. By combining deformation measurements with contact force data, we estimate the underlying mechanical parameters and reconstruct the vegetation's response to interaction. This enables vegetation-aware navigation based on intrinsic environmental properties rather than platform-dependent metrics.

</details>


### [197] [$R^3$: Training Robots to Reason in Natural Language via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26053)
*Lehong Wu,Yuxiao Qu,Zheyuan Hu,Ivan Zhang,Limin Wei,Zackory Erickson,Aviral Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 $R^3$ 的后训练方法，通过自然语言推理来指导机器人操作策略，提升了在长视野任务中的探索与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视觉-语言模型（VLMs）是否能通过自然语言推理来改善机器人在复杂操作任务中的表现，特别是在需要分解、约束跟踪和错误恢复的场景中。

Method: 首先在专家生成的推理轨迹上对VLM进行中段训练，以初始化期望的推理风格，然后利用离线动作数据通过单步基于评分标准的强化学习进一步优化推理器。

Result: $R^3$ 在Language Table和模拟双手 grocery packing 两个基准上显著优于仅依赖指令模仿学习的基线方法，并展现出更好的探索和泛化能力。

Conclusion: 自由形式的语言推理可以作为一种测试时计算机制，有效引导低层策略，提升机器人在长视野操作任务中的性能。

Abstract: Reasoning in language allows foundation models to spend more test-time compute on hard problems, such as those requiring decomposition, constraint tracking, and prediction of future consequences. Whether this mechanism can improve robotic manipulation remains unclear, where long-horizon tasks require tracking partial progress, reasoning about object relations, recovering from mistakes, and steering noisy low-level policies. In this paper, we study whether VLMs can be trained to reason directly in natural language to guide low-level manipulation policies. We introduce $R^3$, a simple post-training recipe that turns off-the-shelf VLMs into robotic reasoners: it first mid-trains a VLM on expert-generated reasoning traces to initialize the desired reasoning style, then improves the reasoner with single-step rubric-based RL from offline action data. Unlike prior robotic reasoning methods that mostly use structured traces as auxiliary supervision, $R^3$ trains free-form language reasoning to produce test-time guidance for action. We instantiate $R^3$ on Language Table and simulated bimanual grocery packing, two controlled testbeds for studying robotic reasoning and long-horizon manipulation. $R^3$ improves exploration and generalization across unseen tasks and significantly outperforms instruction-only imitation learning baselines on both benchmarks. Our analyses suggest that free-form language reasoning can function as a test-time compute mechanism for steering low-level policies. Our project page is available at https://robotic-reasoner.github.io/.

</details>


### [198] [One Policy, Many Embodiments: Unified Camera-Centric Action Geometry Pre-training for Heterogeneous Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26058)
*Xiaomi Embodied Intelligence Team,University of Macau,:,Shaoqing Xu,Fang Li,Guozhi Zhan,Zhixiang Duan,Yuhan Wang,Yuechen Luo,Shengyin Jiang,Hanbing Li,Zhiying Du,Longlong Wang,Longmei Jiang,Weixiang Liang,Ying Gong,Yong Pan,Ziping Zhao,Zhiyuan Chen,Yangwei You,Kun Ma,Qinyuan Liu,Hangjun Ye,Zhi-xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为UCAG-P的相机中心统一动作表示方法，通过将异构的具身数据集映射到共享的几何动作空间，实现跨机器人形态、相机配置和动作空间的通用视觉-语言-动作策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以统一处理不同机器人形态和动作空间的数据，限制了通用策略的学习，因此需要一种能够结构化对齐异构数据的新范式。

Method: 采用相机可观测的锚点运动作为动作表示，结合图像和相机坐标系中的几何信息，设计几何条件动作转换器，将预测动作适配到具体机器人形态。

Result: 在4.03K小时机器人与仿真数据及2.34K小时人类演示数据上训练后，单个模型在多个基准（如LIBERO、RoboTwin等）上取得优异表现，且无需针对特定基准微调。

Conclusion: UCAG-P实现了异构具身数据的统一建模，支持可迁移的操作几何学习，同时保持对特定形态的可控性，推动了通用VLA策略的发展。

Abstract: Scaling generalist vision-language-action (VLA) policies is severely bottlenecked by the inherent heterogeneity of embodied data, which spans diverse robot morphologies, camera configurations, and low-level action spaces. Existing paradigms typically address this mismatch through explicit action retargeting, human-to-robot video synthesis, or dataset-specific adaptation branches, fundamentally hindering the joint learning of a unified policy. We introduce UCAG-P, a camera-centric unified action formulation that structurally aligns heterogeneous embodied datasets into a shared geometric action space. Rather than treating robot-specific commands as the shared policy target, UCAG-P represents manipulation through camera-observable anchor motion in image and camera-frame coordinates, treating robot arms, humanoids, and human hands as different embodiments of a common action schema. A geometry-conditioned action translator combines predicted motion with target-embodiment kinematics to produce executable controls. The resulting decoupled architecture allows a shared VLA policy to learn transferable manipulation geometry while retaining embodiment-specific controllability. UCAG-P is trained on 4.03K hours of robot and simulation data and 2.34K hours of human demonstrations. A single checkpoint reaches 98.3% on LIBERO, 88.7% and 89.2% on RoboTwin Easy and Hard, 82.0% zero-shot on LIBERO-Plus, and 62.0% on RoboCasa GR-1, without benchmark-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [199] [VirTooS: A ROS 2 - Unity Virtualization Toolkit for Fleet Management of Autonomous Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26066)
*Andrea Drudi,Lorenzo Pichierri,Andrea Testa,Giuseppe Notarstefano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种名为VirTooS的Python/C#工具包，用于在自主移动机器人（AMR）团队中实现车队管理任务，结合ROS 2和Unity引擎支持混合现实环境下的虚拟实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了支持在混合现实环境中进行可扩展且逼真的多机器人系统实验，尤其是车队管理与任务分配问题，开发一个灵活、易部署的工具包。

Method: 利用ROS 2和Unity游戏引擎构建虚拟实验环境，集成虚拟与真实传感器（如LiDAR），并通过ChoiRbot框架实现分布式机器人实验；提供容器化部署方案。

Result: 实现了支持虚实融合的机器人仿真与任务分配实验，验证了VirTooS在虚拟环境中进行车队管理的有效性和可扩展性，并支持跨平台快速部署。

Conclusion: VirTooS是一个高效、可定制且易于部署的工具包，为多机器人系统的虚拟与混合现实实验提供了有力支持，具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: In this paper, we present VirTooS, a Python/C# toolkit designed to implement fleet-management tasks on teams of Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs). VirTooS leverages the Robot Operating System (ROS) 2 and Unity game engine to provide realistic, scalable virtual experiments in a mixed-reality environment. The toolbox allows users to easily generate and customize virtual scenarios for realistic simulations. Virtual and real sensors as, e.g., LiDARs, can be exploited to map and safely navigate in the mixed-reality environment. To enable distributed robotics experiments, we propose a set of tailored routines leveraging the ChoiRbot framework. As a motivating example, we show a set of experiments for task assignment problems in a virtual environment, allowing seamless interaction among real and virtual robots. Moreover, the package comes with a containerized suite to easily deploy it on different machines. The source code will be made publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [200] [Gating Before Commitment: Anticipating Intent Divergence to Prevent Post-Interaction Decision Failures in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26074)
*Cong Xu,Ravi Sankar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种语言引导的意图模块，通过计算意图-几何散度得分并在决策层进行门控，有效修复自动驾驶中的规划失败，提前检测异常并抑制误触发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车辆交互中的意图误判导致反复的规划失败，需要一种能够及时识别并纠正错误规划的机制。

Method: 设计一个语言引导的意图模块，读取结构化描述符，计算平滑的意图-几何散度得分，并在走廊包络之前设置门控机制，以决定是否执行规划动作。

Result: 在重放的偏离道路和四段碰撞片段中，该门控机制是唯一能修复规划的层级；主案例中在漂移开始后72ms触发，但早于走廊出口161ms，十次重放均保持轨迹在走廊内；优化后误触发率从每分钟九次降至0.341次；消融实验表明模型在多数故障下检测最快，并能基于不确定性抑制几何规则的误判。

Conclusion: 门控机制有效提升了规划安全性，模型的核心作用是实现最快故障检测并对几何规则提供基于不确定性的否决，从而减少误触发。

Abstract: Intent misinterpretation during vehicle interactions causes recurring planning failures. We study a decision layer in which a language-guided intent module reads structured descriptors, computes a smoothed intent-geometry divergence score, and gates the planned maneuver before commitment, upstream of a corridor envelope. On a replayed off-road departure and four crash clips under a frozen, disclosed implementation, gating is the only layer that repairs the plan: on the main case it fires 72 ms after the drift onset but 161 ms before the corridor exit, keeping the trajectory in the corridor in all ten replays. The first calibration draws nine false triggers in 5.9 minutes, each from scoring uncertainty as half a conflict; a preregistered redesign treating uncertainty as abstention cuts this to 0.341 per minute. Two ablations bound the model's contribution: the full score detects fastest on four of five failures under the deployed eligibility, three of five against the unvetoed rule (000871 by one cycle; 000228 by a pre-onset fire on an uncertain stretch that five clips cannot classify as signal or coincidence; dropping the confidence term costs two detections), while on in-domain tracks at equal false positives the geometric rule more than triples its detection. The evidence supports the gating mechanism; the model's demonstrated roles are the fastest detection on these failures and an uncertainty veto on the geometric rule.

</details>


### [201] [Fast Generative Grasping via Lie Group-Constrained MeanFlow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26076)
*S. Talha Bukhari,Yi Wei,Ruiqi Ni,Zachary Kingston,Aniket Bera*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Lie群约束MeanFlow的快速生成式抓取方法，在保持高性能的同时显著提升推理速度，并在真实机器人场景中无需额外训练即可稳健应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的生成式抓取方法（如扩散模型）虽性能好但推理慢，难以满足机器人实时性需求，因此需要一种快速且可靠的抓取生成方法。

Method: 在SO(3)×R³的乘积李群上结合代数半群一致性条件与黎曼条件流匹配，构建Lie群约束的MeanFlow模型，实现单步或极少步采样生成抓取姿态。

Result: 在ACRONYM数据集上达到与最先进的扩散和流模型相当的抓取生成性能，仅需≤5次网络评估，推理延迟达毫秒级，最快提速39倍，并能在真实环境中鲁棒运行。

Conclusion: 该方法在保证生成质量的前提下极大提升了生成速度，适用于对实时性要求高的机器人操作任务，且具备良好的现实迁移能力。

Abstract: Grasp synthesis is a core task in robotic manipulation, for which the solution typically forms a multimodal distribution rather than a point estimate. Generative robotic grasping aims to learn this distribution with deep generative models such as diffusion and flow-based approaches. The iterative nature of such generative models makes them flexible and generalizable; however, multi-step sampling impedes the time-critical operation required in robotics. We devise an approach to fast generative grasping based on MeanFlow on the product Lie group $\mathcal{G} = \mathrm{SO}(3) \times \mathbb{R}^3$. The training objective couples a purely algebraic semigroup consistency condition with Riemannian Conditional Flow Matching on $\mathcal{G}$ that anchors the average velocity to the data distribution. The resulting Lie Group-constrained MeanFlow formulation samples reliable grasps in $\leq 5$ network evaluations, matching the grasp generation performance of state-of-the-art diffusion and flow-based models on the ACRONYM dataset at millisecond-scale inference latency (up to $39\times$ speed-up). We further demonstrate that the approach directly translates to real-world robotic grasping without additional training or domain adaptation, exhibiting robust grasp synthesis under observation noise.

</details>


### [202] [Zero-WAM: In-Context World-Action Modeling from Human Videos for Open-Ended Task Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26103)
*Jiaming Zhou,Qihang Zhang,Gangwei Xu,Cunxin Fan,Yujie Zhao,Ruilin Wang,Yiming Luo,Shuai Yang,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Junwei Liang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Zero-WAM是一种基于因果视频-动作模型的机器人操作方法，通过上下文中的真人示范视频实现零样本跨任务泛化，在仿真和真实环境中均表现出显著优于基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人学习中零样本跨任务泛化难题，借鉴大语言模型中的上下文学习（ICL）思想，将任务描述从语言转向更富视觉信息的人类操作视频。

Method: 提出Zero-WAM模型，利用自动流水线生成人类-机器人配对数据集HumanGen（74.2K对，涵盖8.6K任务），并引入上下文未来块预测（IFP）目标以增强对视频提示的依赖，提升跨任务泛化能力。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0模拟环境的七个未见任务上，Zero-WAM平均成功率达47.0%，比最强视频-动作基线提高29.5个百分点；在真实世界中也能泛化到多物体、长视野和精细插入等复杂场景。

Conclusion: 将上下文学习范式引入机器人操作是可行且有效的，使用人类视频作为自然任务规范可显著提升零样本跨任务泛化性能。

Abstract: Zero-shot cross-task generalization, where a policy must execute manipulation tasks never seen during training, remains a central challenge in robot learning. In large language models, a novel task can be performed simply by specifying it in the context, without any parameter update. This form of in-context learning (ICL) turns generalization into a problem of task specification. To achieve cross-task generalization, we bring this paradigm to robotic manipulation, and argue that the natural task specification for manipulation is a human video: unlike language, it provides rich visual cues about the intended task evolution. We present Zero-WAM, a causal video-action model that executes unseen tasks by following in-context human video guidance. To address the scarcity of task-rich paired human-robot data, we propose an automatic pipeline that converts task-sampled robot trajectories into semantically matched human videos, yielding HumanGen, a dataset of 74.2K human-robot ICL pairs across 8.6K tasks. For model training, we further introduce an in-context future chunk prediction (IFP) objective that suppresses shortcuts learned from seen tasks and forces the policy to draw task information from the video prompt. On seven unseen tasks in RoboTwin 2.0 simulation, Zero-WAM achieves a 47.0% average success rate, an absolute improvement of 29.5 percentage points over the strongest video-action baseline. In real-world evaluations, it follows human video guidance to generalize to unseen task configurations involving multi-object scenes, long-horizon manipulation, and fine-grained insertion.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [VLM-based automatic multi-granularity graph representation of building layouts for design informatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24886)
*Song Guo,Zhuoshi Chen,Maosu Li,Weimin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于视觉-语言模型的多粒度图表示方法（Level-of-Graphs, LoGs），用于从建筑平面图中自动提取结构化布局信息，支持设计检索与建筑全生命周期管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以为公共建筑平面图构建任务自适应的图表示，限制了设计知识的自动化利用。

Method: 定义多粒度图层次（LoGs），利用视觉-语言模型实现节点识别、边推断、文本解析与图粗化，实现无需人工标注的自动图生成。

Result: 在147个图书馆平面图上验证，生成图与人工标注图节点匹配率≥92%；中粒度图在区域预测上表现最佳（Macro F1=0.647），粗粒度图在布局质量评估中更高效（Spearman's ρ=0.610），且显著降低复杂度。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了可扩展、免标注的平面图知识提取，推动了设计信息学发展，提升了建筑全生命周期中设计信息的利用效率。

Abstract: Architectural floorplan images encode rich relational knowledge among functional spaces, which underpins design retrieval, knowledge-based reasoning, and BIM enrichment through the building lifecycle. However, it remains challenging to automatically construct task-adaptive graph representations for public buildings. To address this gap, we first define a multi-granularity Level-of-Graphs (LoGs) for public building layouts. Methodologically, we present a Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based automatic LoG construction through node identification, edge inference, text parsing, and graph coarsening. VLM-generated representations are systematically evaluated and tested in real-world tasks, using 147 academic library floorplans worldwide as a case study. Experiments showed VLM-generated graphs were broadly consistent with human-labeled graphs (matched node ratio >= 92%; 509.3 s per floor plan for three-LoG graph generation). Meso-grained graphs yield the best node-level zone prediction (Macro F1 = 0.647, at 65% of fine-grained complexity), while coarse-grained graphs are most effective for graph-level layout quality evaluation (Spearman's \r{ho} = 0.610, at 16% of fine-grained complexity). By enabling scalable, annotation-free extraction of structured layout information from floorplan images, this study advances design informatics by converting plan images into knowledge representations, thereby enhancing the utilization of design information across the building life cycle.

</details>


### [204] [SIMGUIDE: Procedurally Grounded Multi-Context Representations for Personalized Agent Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24888)
*Chirag Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SIMGUIDE方法，通过将用户上下文结构化为领域特定的、程序化示例支持的Sims模块，提升AI代理在多情境下的个性化决策能力，并证明表示格式比内容本身更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化AI将用户视为单一整体，无法处理同一用户在不同生活场景中优先级不同甚至冲突的问题；关键不在于缺乏信息，而在于用户表示格式限制了信息的使用。

Method: 设计SIMGUIDE方法，将用户上下文划分为类型化的Sims模块，并用过往决策中的程序性示例来锚定每个约束；构建SIMBENCH测试套件（47个偏好条件规划任务）进行评估，并结合LoRA微调与适配器路由进行参数级验证。

Result: 基于程序性示例的Sims显著优于RAG方法（GPT-4o上+7.9分，p=0.013），并在τ-bench任务上复现优势（p≤0.023）；任务匹配的LoRA提升12.8 ROUGE-L分，按Sim类型路由适配器再增7.3分且对28%路由错误鲁棒。

Conclusion: 用户表示的格式是决定个性化AI性能的首要设计变量，结构化、程序化接地的表示方式能有效支持跨情境的偏好遵循。

Abstract: Personalized AI agents overwhelmingly treat users as single entities: a flat profile concatenated into a prompt. This fails when the same person holds different priorities across life contexts -- and fails catastrophically when those priorities conflict. The core problem is not that agents lack information about users; it is that the format of user representations determines whether an agent can act on that information at all. We introduce SIMGUIDE, a method that structures user context into typed, domain-specific blocks called Sims and grounds each constraint with procedural examples drawn from past decisions. To evaluate this, we construct SIMBENCH, a diagnostic suite of 47 preference-conditioned planning tasks where the correct plan depends on which user context is active -- a property no existing benchmark tests. Declarative Sim constraints alone do not outperform retrieval-based personalization (RAG). Procedurally grounded Sims outperform RAG on GPT-4o (+7.9 Preference Adherence points, $p = 0.013$), and this advantage replicates on 100 $τ$-bench tasks across both GPT-4o and Claude Sonnet~4.5 ($p \leq 0.023$). At the parametric level, the same principle holds: training distribution dominates whether parametric adaptation succeeds at all. Task-matched LoRA fine-tuning improves generation quality by 12.8 ROUGE-L points over the unadapted base model, and routing adapters by Sim type rather than user identity adds a further 7.3 points, robust to 28% routing error. Representation format -- not representation content -- is the first-order design variable.

</details>


### [205] [Reliable LLM-Powered Decision Engines for Large-Scale Supply Chain Operations: Architecture, Safety, and Performance Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24889)
*Nirmal Kumar Jingar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型与数学优化、概率预测和安全约束决策过滤的混合架构——LLM驱动决策引擎（LLM-DE），用于实现更智能、安全且可扩展的供应链决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于规则或纯优化的方法难以应对大规模供应链中的高不确定性、动态性和中断风险，亟需一种能够同时进行推理、适应和优化的智能系统。

Method: 提出LLM-Powered Decision Engine（LLM-DE）混合架构，将大语言模型的语义推理能力与数学优化、概率预测及安全约束决策过滤相结合，构建端到端的决策流程，支持需求预测、库存优化、运输路径规划和中断缓解。

Result: 实验结果表明，该框架相比纯数据驱动或启发式方法，在大规模供应链中能生成更优、更安全且更具可扩展性的决策。

Conclusion: 语言推理与优化及形式化约束的结合为下一代智能供应链基础设施提供了理论与实践基础，能够在高度不确定和复杂环境中可靠运行。

Abstract: Current large-scale supply chains are highly uncertain, dynamic, and disruption prone that are challenging to serve up timely and resilient decisions through traditional rule-based and optimization-only systems. The increasing supply of heterogeneous data sources, such as transactional demand signals and unstructured disruption report, presents a chance of intelligent systems, which could reason, adapt and optimize at the same time. A hybrid architecture that combines large language models (LLMs) with mathematical optimization, probabilistic forecasting, and safety-constrained decision filtering is proposed in this paper as a performance of a Decision Engine, which is called LLM-Powered Decision Engine (LLM-DE). In comparison to purely data-driven or heuristic solutions, LLM-DE integrates semantic reasoning with LLM with a set of performance and safety guarantees that allow safe decision-making in large-scale supply chain processes. The suggested framework enables the end-to-end decision making such as demand forecasting, inventory optimization, and transportation routing and disruption mitigation. The findings affirm that language-based reasoning combined with optimization and formal constraints can be used to come up with not only smarter but also safer and more scalable supply chain decisions. This research provides a new architecture, a complete pipeline of algorithm, and a formulation based on mathematical constructs of the operational decision systems incorporating LLM. The proposed model offers a pragmatic and theoretical basis of the next-generation intelligent supply chain infrastructures that can be implemented to work dependably in the face of uncertainty and massive complexity.

</details>


### [206] [AI-Powered Mental Health Chatbots in Africa: A Systematic Review and Culturally Adaptive Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24890)
*Matshepo Lebese,Pitso Tsibolane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文系统回顾了52项关于AI聊天机器人在心理健康支持中的实证研究，评估其在非洲背景下的文化、语言和基础设施适用性，并提出一个融合非洲文化价值、多语言设计和移动优先的Culturally Adaptive Digital Mental Health (CADMH)框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非洲的心理健康问题因基础设施不足、污名化和专业人员短缺而长期得不到解决，现有AI心理支持工具多基于西方模式，缺乏对非洲本土情境的适应性。

Method: 对2017至2025年间发表的52项实证研究进行系统性综述，分析其在非洲文化、语言和基础设施方面的相关性和适用性。

Result: AI聊天机器人显示出缓解焦虑和抑郁症状、提升心理支持可及性的潜力，但大多数现有系统仍基于西方模型和英语设计，缺乏非洲本地化适配。

Conclusion: 需开发符合非洲文化、语言和数字环境特点的AI心理支持工具，提出的CADMH框架为在非洲整合数字心理健康干预提供了可行路径，有助于提升包容性、可持续性和伦理保障。

Abstract: Mental health challenges in Africa remain under-addressed due to inadequate infrastructure, stigma, and a chronic shortage of professionals. Artificial Intelligence (AI)-powered chatbots are emerging globally as low-cost, accessible tools that can offer psychological support. This paper presents a systematic review of 52 empirical studies published between 2017 and 2025, critically analysing their cultural, linguistic, and infrastructural relevance to African contexts. The findings demonstrate the potential of AI chatbots to improve accessibility, reduce symptoms of anxiety and depression, and expand psychosocial support, yet reveal limited African-specific adaptation. Most systems remain rooted in Western models and English-language designs, leaving critical gaps in local relevance, inclusivity, and sustainability. Based on the findings, the authors develop a Culturally Adaptive Digital Mental Health (CADMH) framework that integrates African cultural values, multilingual design, mobile-first optimisation, and ethical safeguards. The study highlights opportunities and barriers for integrating AI chatbots into African healthcare, offering guidance for research, practice, and policy.

</details>


### [207] [Measurement-Budget Allocation in Quantum Learning with Finite-Shot Generalization Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24891)
*Ferhat Ozgur Catak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在近似量子硬件上进行二元量子分类时，固定测量预算下训练状态数量与每状态采样次数之间的权衡，提出了一个保守的闭式分配规则，并通过理论和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限测量预算下，如何合理分配量子电路的执行次数以有效估计Born概率并优化分类性能是一个关键问题。

Method: 提出了一种分布无关的泛化误差界，分离了有限样本和有限采样的影响，并通过最小化保守的闭式替代目标得到最优分配策略n*和S*，同时在PennyLane上使用变分量子电路进行了仿真验证。

Result: 得到了分配规则n* = 2√(2dB/log(2B/δ)) 和 S* = B/n*，理论误差界为B^(-1/4)；模拟结果显示经验泛化差距始终低于理论界。

Conclusion: 该分配策略为近期量子学习系统的测量预算分配提供了保守但可靠的统计指导，适用于广泛的二元量子测量场景，但自适应含噪训练的扩展仍待解决。

Abstract: On near-term quantum hardware, estimating a Born probability requires repeated circuit executions. A quantum learning experiment with a fixed measurement budget $B$ must therefore decide how many distinct training states $n$ to use and how many shots $S$ to allocate to each state. We study this tradeoff for binary quantum classifiers with fixed or independently selected measurement operators $M$, where the ideal score is $\Tr(Mρ)$. We prove a distribution-free generalization bound that separates the finite-sample and finite-shot contributions. The sample term scales as $\sqrt{d/n}$, while the shot term scales as $\sqrt{(\log n)/S}$; under the constraint $B=nS$, these two terms move in opposite directions. Minimising a conservative closed-form surrogate of the bound gives the allocation rule $\nstar = 2\sqrt{2dB/\log(2B/δ)}$ and $\Sstar = B/\nstar$. This surrogate has the same asymptotic scaling as the exact minimizer and yields a worst-case rate of $B^{-1/4}$. The guarantee is intentionally conservative, since it applies to the full class of binary quantum measurements. We complement the theory with PennyLane simulations using 2-qubit and 4-qubit variational quantum circuits on nine synthetic binary classification benchmarks. In all tested configurations, the one-sided empirical generalization gap remains below the theoretical bound. The result provides a conservative statistical guideline for allocating measurement budgets in finite-shot evaluation and pre-experimental planning for near-term quantum learning systems, complementing hardware-level scheduling and circuit-design considerations. Extending the guarantee to fully adaptive shot-noisy training remains an open problem.

</details>


### [208] [Natural Language Input, Semantic Track Representation, and LLM Inference: Making the Maritime Information Exchange Model Tractable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24892)
*Frederick Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型的实用架构，将自然语言观测自动转化为语义断言记录（SARs），构建知识图谱并进行推理分析，使MIEM和语义轨迹模型在国防与执法中更易部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 消除现有语义轨迹模型因需操作员掌握形式化本体语言和手动编码而难以在国防与执法领域推广的障碍。

Method: 利用大语言模型将操作员的自然语言输入自动翻译为结构化的语义断言记录（SARs），形成知识图谱，并通过第二轮LLM处理实现推理、异常检测与假设排序。

Result: 在两个具体案例（9/11前袭击征兆场景和海上货物检查场景）中验证了从自然语言输入到SAR表示再到推理输出的完整流程，证明该架构可行且高效。

Conclusion: 该架构使得MIEM和语义轨迹模型可立即使用现有技术部署，防止方法被专有系统垄断，并将其建立在连接语义表示与神经流形几何的理论框架之上。

Abstract: We describe a practical architecture for making the Maritime Information Exchange Model (MIEM) and the broader Rich Semantic Track model tractable using current large language model (LLM) technology. The barrier to adoption of semantic track models in defense and law enforcement has been the requirement that operators learn formal ontology languages and manually encode observations as typed logical assertions. We propose eliminating this barrier entirely: operators contribute observations in natural language; an LLM translates these into typed Semantic Assertion Records (SARs), which are named case frames that capture n-ary relations in a single compact structure; a knowledge graph accumulates the SARs; and a second LLM pass performs inference, anomaly detection, and hypothesis ranking over the graph. We work through two detailed examples (a 9/11-era pre-attack indicator scenario and a maritime cargo inspection scenario) showing the full pipeline from natural language input to SAR representation to inference output. We argue that this architecture makes the Track Model and MIEM immediately deployable with current technology, establishes prior art against proprietary enclosure of the approach, and grounds the method in a theoretical framework connecting semantic track representations to neural manifold geometry.

</details>


### [209] [Same-Player Verification for Account Consistency in Counter-Strike 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24893)
*Xuchen Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于行为指纹的同玩家验证方法，用于评估《反恐精英2》（CS2）中玩家账户的行为一致性，通过多场比赛回放数据提取行为特征并训练模型，实现了高精度的玩家身份识别，结果表明低层次操作习惯（如准星控制、射击节奏）比单场表现更具辨识力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在CS2等竞技射击游戏中，存在代打、排位提升和高技能玩家使用低排名账号等问题，需要判断当前游戏行为是否与其历史行为一致，传统人工审核效率低，因此需要自动化的方法来进行账户行为一致性验证。

Method: 将同玩家验证任务建模为成对行为比对问题，从比赛回放（demo）中提取每个玩家的行为轨迹，构建包含准星控制、移动-停止-射击协调、经济购买、战斗参与和时间节奏等多个维度的行为指纹，并基于这些指纹训练一个成对比较的监督学习模型进行身份一致性判断。

Result: 基于1,330个CS2比赛回放提取出13,300个玩家行为样本，构建了8840万候选对中的663,590个正负样本对；最终模型在ROC AUC上达到0.931，在95%精确率下不同玩家召回率为0.722；特征分析显示最低层操作（尤其是准星控制、射击节奏和移动-停止-射击协调）最具识别性；通过聚合最多10场历史比赛（K=10），AUC进一步提升至0.986。

Conclusion: CS2的比赛回放数据可以有效支持基于监督学习的同玩家行为验证，通过多场比赛的历史聚合可显著提升账户级身份一致性建模的准确性，尤其依赖于稳定的低层次操作习惯作为身份标识。

Abstract: In competitive first-person shooter (FPS) games such as Counter-Strike 2 (CS2), account-integrity review often asks whether an account's recent behavior remains consistent with its historical operator. This consistency question arises in cases such as temporary substitution, rank boosting, and high-skill players using lower-ranked accounts, where manual review requires comparing a current match against multiple historical matches. We formulate this review task as same-player verification: we encode the behavioral trajectory of a single player in a match replay (demo) as a demo-player behavioral fingerprint, and train a model to judge whether two behavioral observations come from the same real player. Grounded in CS2 game understanding, the fingerprints cover crosshair control, movement-stop-fire coordination, economy/buy, combat/engagement, and temporal rhythm. From 1,330 CS2 demos we extract 13,300 demo-player observations, and sample 663,590 same/different pairs from an 88.4M candidate-pair space for supervised training and evaluation. The final pairwise model reaches an average ROC AUC of 0.931 and achieves 0.722 different-player recall at 95% precision. Feature analysis shows that the strongest identity signals come from low-level operations, especially crosshair control, firing rhythm, and movement-stop-fire coordination, indicating that stable low-level mechanical habits are more informative for this verification task than single-match performance outcomes. In the account-history aggregation evaluation, increasing history depth raises AUC from the K=1 single-pair baseline of 0.931 to 0.986 at K=10. These results show that CS2 demo behavior can support supervised same-player verification and account-level identity-consistency modeling through multi-demo history aggregation.

</details>


### [210] [Semantic Graph Unification for Industrial Digital Threads: Bridging 11 Heterogeneous Manufacturing Systems Through Ontology-Driven Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24918)
*Grama Chethan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于本体驱动的语义图框架，用于统一制造业中异构系统的数字线程，通过RDF知识图谱集成多源数据，并利用自动化发现引擎跨系统边界挖掘洞察，实验证明该方法显著提升了工业数据分析的完整性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代制造企业存在ERP、MES、PLM等异构系统，数据孤岛严重阻碍了整体分析、根因追溯和工业4.0的实现，传统的点对点集成方式扩展性差且脆弱，亟需一种可扩展、标准化的语义集成方案。

Method: 构建一个基于标准（如ISA-95、OPC UA等）的工业本体，设计五阶段ETL管道将11个模拟数据源映射为RDF三元组，建立包含78个类、108个对象属性和243个数据属性的知识图谱，并通过owl:sameAs实现自动实体对齐；在此基础上开发自动化发现引擎，采用九类策略进行跨系统分析，并通过SPARQL接口和Model Context Protocol向LLM代理开放图谱能力。

Result: 实验表明，阻断24个跨系统工具会导致召回率从1.00降至0.31（F1从1.00降至0.48），证明69%的可发现信号依赖跨系统连接；留一法消融实验确认九种策略中有六种提供独特信号；在作者构建的65个信号清单上验证达到F1=1.00（95% CI [0.79, 1.00]），但未进行独立外部验证。

Conclusion: 基于本体的语义图统一框架能有效打破制造系统间的数据孤岛，支持跨域关联分析与自动化洞察发现，具备在多个制造行业（如航空航天、医药、快消等）推广的模式稳定性，是实现工业4.0数字线程的关键基础设施。

Abstract: Modern manufacturing enterprises operate heterogeneous systems -- ERP, MES, PLM, SCADA, QMS, SCM -- each with its own data model and API. The resulting silos prevent holistic analysis, delay root-cause investigation, and obstruct Industry 4.0 traceability. Point-to-point integration scales as O(n^2) and accumulates brittle dependencies. This paper presents an open framework for semantic graph unification of industrial digital threads. An ontology-driven RDF knowledge graph unifies data from 11 simulated sources across nine domains through a five-stage ETL pipeline with automated entity resolution spanning 97 owl:sameAs identity links. The ontology encompasses 78 RDFS classes, 108 object properties, and 243 data properties, drawing on ISA-95, OPC UA, eClass, the Asset Administration Shell, RAMI 4.0, and additional standards. An automated discovery engine applies nine strategy categories -- cross-station correlation, alarm coverage, ECN impact, CUSUM/EWMA drift detection -- to surface insights spanning system boundaries. The primary empirical result: blocking 24 cross-system tools reduces recall from 1.00 to 0.31 (F1 from 1.00 to 0.48), showing that 69% of discoverable signals require cross-system graph joins. Leave-one-out ablation confirms six of nine strategies contribute unique signals. Verification against a 65-signal manifest (16 positive, 49 null) yields F1 = 1.00 (95% Clopper-Pearson CI [0.79, 1.00]); as the manifest was author-constructed, this constitutes verification not independent validation. The graph is exposed to LLM agents via 287 Model Context Protocol tools as a SPARQL-native semantic layer. Five industry templates (aerospace, CPG, pharma, medical devices, turbine blades) demonstrate schema stability across manufacturing verticals.

</details>


### [211] [post-graph-rag: A PostgreSQL-Native Graph RAG Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24921)
*Chandan Rajah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: post-graph-rag 是一个开源的图增强生成引擎，通过统一存储和优化信息提取，解决了现有方法在基础设施、图质量和时间演化上的三大问题，在关系密度、查询效率和时态支持上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的检索增强生成系统在基础设施一致性、图谱质量（如噪声边过多）和时间演化支持方面存在显著问题，导致维护成本高且效果受限。

Method: post-graph-rag 将文本块、实体图和社区摘要统一存储于一个 PostgreSQL 数据库中，利用 pgvector 进行搜索，边表进行遍历；引入提取时不变规则过滤低质量信息，标准化谓词，实体消解，并支持否定关系和时间有效性管理，实现关系的更新与 as-of 查询。

Result: 在相同提取与嵌入模型下，相比 LightRAG，post-graph-rag 构建的图关系密度最高达 2.4 倍，边标签更简洁（0.46–0.58 vs 0.77–1.33），查询延迟更低，并能识别出 13 和 8 个被后续文档取代的关系，而基线为零。

Conclusion: post-graph-rag 通过工程整合与质量控制，在不增加基础设施复杂度的前提下提升了图 RAG 的密度、精度和时间感知能力，验证了其在实际应用中的优势。

Abstract: Graph-based retrieval-augmented generation connects facts that no single passage states, but current implementations pay for that three times: in infrastructure, requiring a vector store, graph database and document store to be kept consistent; in graph quality, because an extraction pipeline that never refuses output fills the graph with edges that assert nothing; and over time, because a graph that only accumulates treats superseded and current facts alike.
  post-graph-rag is an open-source engine addressing all three. Text chunks with embeddings, a canonical entity graph and community summaries live in one PostgreSQL database, with pgvector for search and edge tables for traversal. Extraction-time invariants run before anything is written: vague predicates, pronominal names and bare quantities are rejected; predicates are normalised onto an optional vocabulary; entities resolve to one vertex per canonical name via model-supplied aliases; and denied relations keep the positive predicate under a negation flag. A temporal layer lets relations carry a validity period from the prose, lets a later document supersede an earlier incompatible assertion from document order alone, and answers as-of queries.
  Against LightRAG on three corpora under identical extraction and embedding models, post-graph-rag builds a denser graph everywhere, up to $2.4\times$ the relations per entity, and a more queryable one: distinct edge labels run at 0.46 to 0.58 per relation, 0.11 under a controlled vocabulary, against 0.77 to 1.33. It answers comparably with lower query latency, and supports temporal evolution the baseline lacks: 13 and 8 relationships superseded on a novel sequence and a decade of filings, against zero. These are engineering measurements, not a benchmark result.
  Code: https://github.com/crajah/post-graph-rag, https://github.com/crajah/post-graph

</details>


### [212] [FrontierChallenge: Evaluating Scientific Workflow Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24979)
*Liangcai Su,Zhaopeng Feng,Zhuo Chen,Zhen Zhang,Xiang Lin,Ruilin Li,Handuo Zhang,Ning Wang,Kailong Wen,Yueqi Guo,Feng Xing,Yiling Guo,Chenxiong Qian,Simon Shaolei Du,Lidong Bing,Xinyu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FrontierChallenge是一个跨领域基准测试，包含300个端到端科学工作流任务，评估结果显示当前前沿模型在完整交付科学任务方面表现不佳，高分或完成声明不能可靠反映任务真正完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试多关注最终答案、孤立程序或单一领域，缺乏对端到端科学工作流执行和科研成果完整性的综合评估，因此需要构建更全面的评测基准。

Method: 提出FrontierChallenge基准测试，涵盖97个已发布的跨学科科学任务，评估12种前沿模型与3种代理架构的组合表现，采用通过率（Pass Rate）和平均得分（Avg. Score）衡量性能。

Result: 最佳配置仅完成97项任务中的20项（通过率20.6%），部分领域如分析化学和电化学/环境虽有高平均分（87.6和94.9），但通过率极低（4%和0%），且75.5%未通过的Claude Code轨迹仍以声称完成结束。

Conclusion: 高部分得分或自信的完成声明并不能可靠表明科学任务已被完全交付，必须同时评估端到端工作流执行和科研成果的完整性。

Abstract: Scientific agents increasingly analyze data, execute code, and produce research artifacts, yet most benchmarks emphasize final answers, isolated programs, or a single domain. We introduce FrontierChallenge, a cross-domain benchmark comprising 300 end-to-end scientific workflows. In this paper, we release and evaluate 97 of these tasks, spanning quantum chemistry, molecular dynamics, materials characterization, analytical chemistry, life science, and electrochemistry/environment. Each task provides fixed inputs and specifies a bundle of required scientific deliverables. We evaluate twelve frontier models with three agent scaffolds. Pass Rate measures the fraction of tasks satisfying the full-completion criterion, while Avg. Score captures partial progress. Each of the best-performing configurations completed only 20 of the 97 released tasks, yielding a Pass Rate of 20.6%. Partial progress translated especially poorly into complete delivery in analytical chemistry and electrochemistry/environment: Avg. Scores reached 87.6 and 94.9, but the highest Pass Rates were only 4% and 0%. Among non-passing Claude Code trajectories, 75.5% still ended with language claiming completion. These findings show that neither high partial scores nor confident claims of completion reliably indicate that a scientific task has been fully delivered, highlighting the need to evaluate end-to-end workflow execution and the completeness of scientific deliverables together.

</details>


### [213] [Solving Robust POMDPs with Omega-regular Objectives via Partially Observable Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24986)
*Durgam Latha,Dion Reji,S. Akshay,Djordje Zikelic,Shankaranarayanan Krishna*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在未知精确转移概率的情况下，如何求解具有omega-regular目标的鲁棒POMDP（RPOMDP），并建立了其与部分可观测随机博弈（POSG）之间的等价性，从而推导出新的计算复杂性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于在实际应用中难以获得精确的转移概率，因此需要研究在不确定性环境下如何有效求解POMDP问题，特别是面对复杂的时序逻辑目标。

Method: 通过将(s,a)-矩形且具有多面体不确定集的RPOMDP转化为具有omega-regular目标的部分可观测随机博弈（POSG），并证明两者之间存在双向归约，从而建立语义等价性。

Result: 首次证明了RPOMDP与POSG在omega-regular目标下的双向归约和语义等价性，并基于此得到了解决不同omega-regular目标下RPOMDP的新计算复杂性上下界结果，同时推导出RMDPs的新复杂性结果。

Conclusion: RPOMDPs在(s,a)-矩形和多面体不确定集条件下与POSG等价，该理论框架为分析和求解具有复杂目标的鲁棒决策问题提供了新途径，并丰富了相关计算复杂性理论。

Abstract: Robust POMDPs (RPOMDPs) generalize classical POMDPs to the setting where exact transition probabilities are not known -- rather, they are only known to belong to some uncertainty set of values. In this work, we study the problem of solving RPOMDPs with general omega-regular objectives, which subsume a broad class of objectives such as reachability, safety, and linear temporal logic (LTL) objectives. We show that, for (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets, the problem of solving RPOMDPs under omega-regular objectives can be reduced to solving partially observable stochastic games (POSGs) under omega-regular objectives. Moreover, we show for the first time that reductions can be constructed in both directions, establishing the semantic equivalence between (s,a)-rectangular RPOMDPs with polytopic uncertainty sets and POSGs. This allows us to derive a range of new computational complexity results, including both upper and lower complexity bounds, on solving RPOMDPs with different omega-regular objectives. As a corollary, we also derive new computational complexity results for RMDPs.

</details>


### [214] [CVE-SAI: Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing for Risk-Controlled E-commerce Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25023)
*Xiaolong Sun,Qichao Wang,Hangyu Li,Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CVE-SAI的新方法，用于多模态电商属性填充，通过分离推理与索引准入决策，并引入视觉证据引导机制，在保证视觉支持的前提下提升属性推断和检索性能，同时有效控制错误传播风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在优化属性预测准确性时忽视了对视觉支持的验证，混淆了临时预测与持久索引录入，且缺乏对事实错误或无视觉依据值的风险控制。为此，本文旨在构建一个能确保视觉可解释性并控制错误索引风险的属性索引系统。

Method: 提出CVE-SAI框架：首先仅基于主图像和属性问题推断并冻结本体约束的候选值；然后通过聚焦区扭曲（FZD）构建属性特定的视觉依赖代理，利用证据引导注意力重分配（EGAR）优化评分；最后对候选值进行多重审计（证据必要性、保留性、稳定性及文本冲突检查），仅允许目录文本收紧准入而不修改候选值，并采用独立族级校准选择策略以满足5%不安全准入预算下的有限样本单边界。

Result: 在Amazon Berkeley Objects的五个视觉属性上实验表明，CVE-SAI在属性推断、证据定位、认证准入覆盖率和受控检索性能方面均优于现有方法，实现了最低的自动诱导不安全暴露水平。

Conclusion: 将属性推理与索引准入分离，并结合反事实视觉证据引导和严格准入审计，能够有效提升多模态产品模型的可靠性与检索效果，防止错误信息持续污染索引。

Abstract: Multimodal product models can complete missing e-commerce attributes, yet current methods still optimize attribute-answer accuracy without verifying visual support, conflate transient prediction with persistent index admission, and lack explicit risk control over factually incorrect or visually unsupported values. We address these gaps with Counterfactual Visual Evidence-Guided Selective Attribute Indexing (CVE-SAI), which first infers and freezes an ontology-constrained candidate from the primary image and attribute question without catalog text, and then decides whether that candidate should enter the index. Focus-Zone Distortion (FZD) constructs an attribute-specific visual-dependence proxy through a controlled counterfactual intervention, and Evidence-Guided Attention Redistribution (EGAR) uses the proxy to refine ontology-constrained scoring. The canonical candidate is frozen before evidence necessity, evidence retention, nuisance-transformation stability, and candidate-specific catalog-text conflict audits; catalog text can only tighten admission and cannot revise the candidate. Independent family-level calibration selects one policy with a simultaneous one-sided finite-sample bound under a 5% unsafe-admission budget. Experiments on five visual attributes derived from Amazon Berkeley Objects show that CVE-SAI improves attribute inference and evidence localization, achieves the highest certified admission coverage under the shared risk protocol, and yields the strongest controlled retrieval performance with the lowest unsafe auto-induced exposure among automatic-admission systems. Separating inference from admission therefore enables visually supported attribute completion to improve retrieval while limiting persistent index contamination.

</details>


### [215] [Retrieve, Match, Escalate: Accurate and Scalable Product Linking with VLM-Distilled Cross-Encoders and Agentic VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25037)
*Jian Wang,Steven Xu,Sanjyot Thete,Maryam Barouti,Tom Tang,Elaine Wu,Charu Sareen,Kyle MacDonald*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种级联式的商品链接方法，通过检索-匹配框架高效地将商家商品记录映射到标准产品目录，利用轻量级交叉编码器处理简单案例，多模态视觉语言模型代理解决复杂案例，显著提升覆盖率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模市场场景中，传统单一模型难以兼顾高精度和计算效率，尤其面对数十亿噪声数据和数千万标准产品的匹配任务，需更智能的分级处理机制。

Method: 采用检索-匹配级联架构：先用检索模块筛选候选匹配，轻量级文本交叉编码器自动解析高置信度样本，多模态视觉语言模型代理通过分析图像和网络搜索解决模糊情况；交叉编码器通过双VLM共识标签蒸馏训练，无需人工标注。

Result: 交叉编码器在98%精度下实现68%端到端链接覆盖率，结合仅对困难样本调用的代理模型后，覆盖率提升至77%，代理模型以约七分之一成本达到接近闭源前沿VLM的精度（88% vs 92%）。

Conclusion: 该级联方法根据实例难度动态分配计算资源，在保证高精度的同时大幅降低整体计算成本，适用于大规模、多品类的商品链接任务。

Abstract: Product linking, the entity-resolution task of mapping merchant product records to canonical catalog products, consolidates fragmented listings so downstream search, recommendation, and advertising see one clean entry per product. At marketplace scale, billions of noisy, multi-category records must be resolved against tens of millions of canonical products, where scoring every candidate with a single model is either too weak for the hard cases or too costly for the easy ones. We present a production retrieve-then-match cascade that spends computation in proportion to difficulty: retrieval surfaces plausible matches, a lightweight text cross-encoder auto-resolves the high-confidence majority, and an agentic multimodal vision-language model settles the ambiguous remainder by inspecting product images and issuing web searches for evidence that is in neither record. The cross-encoder is distilled from millions of dual-VLM-consensus labels, retiring human annotation from the training set, and is calibrated to auto-accept links at a 98% precision bar validated against a smaller operator-certified audit. The agent is a self-hosted open-weight model that reaches a closed frontier VLM's precision at a four-point recall cost (88% versus 92%) for roughly one-seventh the per-pair cost, with no fine-tuning. Per-pair cost spans nearly five orders of magnitude from the cheap cross-encoder to the frontier VLM, so escalating only the hard tail to the agent raises end-to-end link coverage from the cheap stage's 68% to 77%.

</details>


### [216] [LifePlanner: Evaluating LLM Agents for Geo-spatial Planning with Social Media Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25039)
*Zhen Dong,Yuning Peng,Yutao Shi,Lei Zhong,Yongsen Mao,Yuan Liu,Haiping Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LifePlanner是一个引入大规模本地社交媒体数据的地理空间规划评测基准，旨在评估LLM代理在复杂、现实约束下的规划能力，揭示当前模型在证据获取、工具使用和约束整合方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间规划基准多依赖干净的地图数据，缺乏真实世界中的开放性社会信号，无法充分测试LLM代理在现实场景中的工具使用与多约束推理能力。

Method: 构建LifePlanner基准，融合地图数据与大规模本地社交媒体帖子，并通过MCP工具集提供访问；设计涵盖四类任务、三个难度级别的评估套件，系统测试前沿LLM的表现。

Result: 实验显示，前沿LLM在简单检索任务上表现良好，但在复杂规划任务中性能显著下降（通过率降至40.2%）；失败主因是证据获取不全、工具使用不精确和约束整合能力弱，而非模型规模或推理长度。

Conclusion: 提升LLM在现实规划任务中的表现需聚焦于更有效的具身规划机制，而非单纯依赖模型扩展。

Abstract: Geo-spatial planning, like trip design, is a realistic testbed for LLM agents because it requires grounded tool use, noisy evidence retrieval, and multi-constraint reasoning. Most benchmarks, however, only provide clean geospatial data and tools, missing the open-ended social signals that people use in daily planning. We introduce LifePlanner, a benchmark that enriches map data with large-scale local social media posts and provides access through an MCP toolset. LifePlanner provides an evaluation suite spanning four task categories and three difficulty levels. Experiments show frontier LLMs perform well on simple retrieval but degrade sharply on complex planning, with the Pass Rate dropping to 40.2%. Results show that failures mainly stem from incomplete evidence acquisition from such a large multimodal database, imprecise tool use, and weak constraint integration rather than model size or reasoning length, suggesting that future progress requires effective grounded planning instead of scaling alone.

</details>


### [217] [SimVerity: When Does Simulated Agent Success Survive Physical Deployment?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25067)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Yefan Zhang,Krinos Li,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SimVerity是一个用于评估AI代理在模拟测试中表现与实际部署一致性的验证框架，通过在真实环境中重放匹配场景并结合物理证据交叉验证，发现模拟测试可能掩盖现实中的短暂失败，从而提供更可靠的部署决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟测试广泛用于评估AI代理，但其结果能否可靠反映实际部署表现尚未被系统量化，因此需要一种能够衡量模拟 verdict 向现实转移有效性的框架。

Method: 提出SimVerity框架，通过在目标智能家居部署中重放匹配的模拟场景，并利用独立校准的物理见证（如摄像头）进行跨验证，分析执行过程中状态、效果和结果的一致性。

Result: 高级模拟器虽通过全部240次灯光测试，但摄像头捕捉到42次亚秒级故障；基于风险画像可预测未在物理路径上测量的故障，且模型配置更改显著提升场景匹配率；第二个模拟器未能提供额外交叉验证，仅物理测量能揭示共性盲点。

Conclusion: 部署成功是动态过程而非静态属性，SimVerity将 verdict 转移转化为明确的部署前决策（通过、 abstain 或升级），提升了AI代理评估的可信度和审计能力。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is widely used to benchmark AI agents, yet how much evidence a simulated pass provides about physical deployment has not been systematically quantified. We present SimVerity, a verdict-transfer assurance framework: it replays matched scenarios on target smart home deployments and cross-validates agent execution against independently qualified physical witnesses. Our evaluation highlights that deployment success is a real-world process, not a static property in simulation: completion, reported state, observable effect, and settled outcome diverged within the same execution. Although an advanced simulator cleared all 240 light trials, a camera caught 42 sub-second failures invisible to settled-state checks. False clearance was predictable: a risk profile learned from measured trials and locked before evaluation predicted failures on a path it never physically measured, beating a property-blind baseline in all eleven held-out sessions across two cohorts. Agent auditability was also measurable: switching one agent loop's model-client/serving configuration raised its scenario-matching share from 52-88% to 100%. Finally, a second qualified simulator added no independent cross-check: it never disagreed on any overlapping case, and only physical measurement exposed their shared blind spots. SimVerity turns verdict transfer into an explicit decision: clear, abstain, or escalate before deployment.

</details>


### [218] [Auto-Policy, not Auto-Skill: Compiled Agent Skills for the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25091)
*Zhonghao Zhan,Hamed Haddadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Borrowed Authority安全问题，即恶意或误用的技能（Skill）可通过借用权限导致物理伤害，现有系统缺乏对权限调用的有效控制。为此，作者提出Edge Skillguard——一种内置于技能构件中的类型化授权保护层，通过世界状态和传感器证据的检查阻止非法请求，在实验中成功拦截了所有60个攻击尝试且不影响正常操作，建议高风险技能应将可验证的调用策略与程序知识共同封装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自治代理系统专注于生成更多技能以提升效率，但忽视了安全性核心问题：技能描述行为方式，而策略决定哪些行为可执行。现有方法将权限决策完全交给模型，导致恶意技能可能通过借用权限引发物理危害，亟需一种机制来确保物理动作依赖于机器可验证的证据而非代理间的信任声明。

Method: 提出Edge Skillguard，一种嵌入在技能构件内部的类型化授权层，不同于传统工作流引擎位于工具之间，它结合世界状态和传感器证据设置防护规则，在边缘控制平面测试平台上验证其有效性，并测试多种攻击变体及跨主机扩展性。

Result: 在真实边缘控制平面测试中，Edge Skillguard成功拦截全部60个 borrowed-authority 请求（五种攻击变体），未阻断任何良性请求，结果在5倍负载和通过Tailscale网络的多主机环境下依然成立。

Conclusion: 高风险技能应将类型化的调用策略与程序性知识一同封装，使物理动作依赖于机器可检查的证据而非其他代理的权限声明，从而填补当前自主代理系统中的关键安全缺口。

Abstract: Self-evolving Skill harnesses (AutoSkills, Hermes Agent) generate more advisory orchestration automatically; their reported gains are efficiency, not safety. This misses the actual gap: a Skill describes how an agent should behave; a Policy decides which behavior is allowed to become an action. Today's format covers the first with markdown and scripts; the second is left to the model. Generating more Skills scales the gap, not the safety, especially when a wrong invocation can unlock a door or move money. Two adjacent attacks are documented: malicious skills compromising cloud software, and jailbroken LLM-controlled robots causing physical harm. Their intersection, malicious agent skills causing physical harm, follows directly but has not been reported. We name this class Borrowed Authority: Skills format gives the receiving agent no typed way to reject an inter-agent permission claim, so a malicious or misused Skill can drive actuation by attaching one. We propose Edge Skillguard, a typed authority layer that lives inside the Skill artifact rather than between tools as workflow engines do, with guards over world state and sensor evidence. On a live edge control-plane testbed, the guards reject 60/60 borrowed-authority requests across five attack variants without blocking benign requests, and the result holds at 5x scale and across hosts over a Tailscale mesh. These results suggest that high-risk Skills should co-package typed invocation policy with procedural knowledge, so that physical actions depend on machine-checkable evidence rather than peer-agent claims.

</details>


### [219] [PhysElite: How Far Are LLMs from Solving Olympiad-Level Physics Problems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25097)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Liyunfeng Chen,Aixin Shi,Haoyu Cheng,Zixiang Fang,Yiqiang Zou,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了PhysElite，一个大规模双语多模态物理奥林匹克级别推理基准，包含11,586道高难度题目及其对应的图表、中英双语逐步解答和最终答案，用于评估多模态大语言模型在复杂物理问题求解中的表现。实验表明当前最强模型的准确率仅为33.7%，并通过逐级分析揭示了模型在推理链中的薄弱环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理评测基准在高难度数据集和视觉形式、知识点覆盖以及解题过程记录方面存在不足，难以全面反映大语言模型在专家级物理推理中的真实能力，因此需要构建更全面、更具挑战性的评测基准。

Method: 构建了一个名为PhysElite的大规模双语多模态物理问题数据集，涵盖大量奥林匹克级别题目，并为每道题提供视觉图表、中英双语逐步推理解答和标准答案；对18种开源与闭源多模态大语言模型进行整体答案准确率和逐步骤推理过程的评估。

Result: 当前最强模型在PhysElite上的答案准确率仅为33.7%；通过逐步骤评估发现模型在多个推理环节存在显著失败，暴露出其在复杂物理推理中的局限性。

Conclusion: PhysElite能有效评估多模态大语言模型在高难度物理问题上的推理能力，揭示了现有模型在处理专家级科学推理任务时仍有巨大提升空间，推动未来研究关注更深层次的因果和逻辑推理机制。

Abstract: Understanding how (multimodal) large language models perform on physics problems requires benchmarks that reflect the difficulty and breadth of expert-level physical reasoning. Existing physics benchmarks remain limited in the following two important ways: (1) short of high-difficulty datasets, and (2) lack of comprehensive coverage of visual forms, knowledge points, and step-by-step solution processes. As a result, model performance on current datasets may not be fully representative of their ability to solve complex physics problems. To address these issues, we present PhysElite, a large-scale bilingual multimodal benchmark for Olympiad-level physics reasoning. PhysElite contains 11,586 Olympiad-tier problems. For each problem, we provide corresponding visual diagrams, step-by-step bilingual Chinese-English solution derivations, and the final answer. We benchmark 18 open-source and closed-source MLLMs, and find that even the strongest model reaches only 33.7% answer accuracy. We additionally conduct step-level process evaluation to diagnose where models fail in the reasoning chain. Our datasets are released at https://huggingface.co/datasets/physelite/PhysElite.

</details>


### [220] [Towards Reliable, Generalizable, and Specific In-Context Knowledge Editing via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25100)
*Xuzhong Wang,Maiqi Jiang,Tejal Nair,Girija Bhusal,Yanfu Zhang,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MO-IKE的多目标上下文知识编辑方法，通过强化学习优化提示构造过程中的可靠性、泛化性和特异性之间的平衡，在Llama-3.2上显著提升了知识编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文知识编辑方法通常只优化单一目标且未能将提示视为具有结构的整体，忽略了不同目标间的权衡和演示的全局组织。

Method: 提出Multi-Objective In-context Knowledge Editing (MO-IKE)，将提示构建建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程，使用多目标强化学习训练动态检索器以同时优化可靠性、通用性和特异性。

Result: 在Llama-3.2模型上，MO-IKE将编辑成功率从85.0%提升至92.0%，转述一致性从77%提升至79%，保留率提高了23.0%。

Conclusion: MO-IKE通过多目标优化实现了更平衡和全局一致的提示构建，显著优于先前的基于强化学习的方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful but limited by static parametric knowledge that becomes outdated once pretraining ends. Knowledge editing addresses this problem by updating model behavior on target facts without full retraining. In particular, in-context knowledge editing has gained attention because it is training-free and readily applicable to black-box LLMs. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based approaches improve over fixed retrieval strategies by adapting prompt construction to the quantity-quality trade-off. Despite initial success, they fail to model the prompt as a structured entity under the distinct and often competing objectives of reliability, generality, and specificity. Previous methods largely optimize a single objective and make decisions over only part of the prompt construction process, thereby overlooking both the balance of different objectives and the global organization of demonstrations. We propose Multi-Objective In-context Knowledge Editing (MO-IKE), a multi-objective RL algorithm that formulates prompt construction for in-context knowledge editing as a Constrained Markov Decision Process. MO-IKE trains a dynamic retriever to optimize competing objectives in knowledge editing, enabling more balanced and globally coherent prompt construction. On Llama-3.2, MO-IKE improves edit success (reliability) from 85.0% to 92.0%, paraphrase consistency (generality) from 77% to 79%, while increasing retention rate (specificity) by 23.0% compared to prior RL-based methods.

</details>


### [221] [FuzzingBrain-Bench V1: Evaluating Open-Ended Bug Discovery by LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25158)
*Ze Sheng,Aleksandar Kezic,Zhicheng Chen,Jeff Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FuzzingBrain-Bench是一个用于评估AI模型在开源软件中发现漏洞能力的新基准，通过生成触发不同崩溃的输入来衡量模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常要求模型生成触发预定义漏洞的概念验证输入，但可能忽略模型发现的其他有效崩溃，无法真实反映其能力。

Method: 提供一个包含开源项目和检测工具插装harness的Docker镜像，要求模型生成能触发尽可能多独特崩溃的输入，根据崩溃签名数量、上限和难度系数评分。

Result: FuzzingBrain-Bench V1包含77个挑战，来自43个开源项目；Claude Opus 4.8表现最佳，触发了60个挑战的崩溃，得分为196/579，有13个挑战无任何模型触发崩溃。

Conclusion: 该基准更全面地评估了大语言模型在软件漏洞发现方面的真实能力，揭示了当前模型的潜力与局限。

Abstract: Evaluating the ability of large language models (LLMs) to discover software bugs is increasingly important. Existing benchmarks typically evaluate this capability by asking the model to generate a proof-of-concept input that triggers a predefined target vulnerability. However, this setup may overlook valid crashes discovered by the model when they do not match the predefined target. As a result, the evaluation may not reflect the model's real capability.
  We present FuzzingBrain-Bench, a benchmark for assessing AI models' ability to discover bugs in open-source software. Models are given an open-source project and a sanitizer-instrumented harness in a self-contained Docker image. Their goal is to generate inputs that trigger as many distinct crashes as possible through the harness. A model's performance on each challenge is scored based on the number of distinct crash signatures it produces, capped at a predefined maximum and weighted by a difficulty coefficient.
  FuzzingBrain-Bench V1 consists of 77 challenges drawn from 43 open-source projects, with 36 C, 32 C++, and 9 Java/JVM challenges. We evaluate Claude Haiku 4.5, Claude Sonnet 4.6, and Claude Opus 4.8 on the full benchmark. Claude Opus 4.8 performs best, triggering crashes in 60 of 77 challenges and achieving a score of 196 out of 579. None of the three models triggers a crash in 13 challenges. The FuzzingBrain-Bench corpus and harnesses are publicly available at https://github.com/fuzzingbrain/FuzzingBrain-Bench.

</details>


### [222] [Tunable Tool-Call Rates in LLM Agents via Representation Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25198)
*Yuqi Chen,Vincent Siu,Yang Liu,Dawn Song,Chenguang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的线性控制方法，通过在残差流中引入单一方向向量来精确调控大语言模型的工具调用行为，实现了调用率从接近0%到超过90%的单调调节，并在多种模型架构上实现了跨工具泛化和准确率显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在工具调用决策上表现不佳，存在过度使用或使用不足的问题，现有方法如后训练和提示工程成本高且难以在推理时调整，因此需要一种更高效、灵活的控制机制。

Method: 利用模型自身对工具使用的偏好信号，在残差流中提取一个线性方向，作为推理时的干预手段，通过调节该方向的强度α来控制工具调用概率，无需修改提示或进行额外训练。

Result: 该方法能够单调调节工具调用率（从接近0%至90%以上），抑制或激发合理的工具调用，适用于未见工具并具有良好泛化性；在实际工具执行中，单次调节即可逼近成本与准确率的帕累托前沿，开放域问答准确率几乎翻倍（0.29→0.56），且在密集、MoE和多模态模型上均有效。

Conclusion: 通过一个无需训练的线性方向即可有效控制大语言模型的工具调用倾向，提供了一种通用、高效、可推广的推理时干预机制，显著提升了工具使用效率和模型性能。

Abstract: Deciding whether to call a tool is a core competence of an LLM agent, and a costly one to get wrong: needless calls add latency, accrue cost, and may trigger irreversible side effects, while missing calls leave the model confidently wrong on questions it could only answer through tool-calls. Models manage this balance poorly, both over-using and under-using tools. Existing methods such as post-training and prompt engineering are expensive and difficult to modify at inference time. We show that whether an instruction-tuned model calls a tool can be controlled by a single linear direction in its residual stream, extracted without any training from the model's own tool-use preference signal and turned into an inference-time intervention with no prompt change. Adding the direction with strength $α$ moves the call rate monotonically from near $0\% $ to over $90\%$ while keeping calls well-formed.
